```
47	export interface DropRarity { legendary: boolean; veryrare: boolean; rare: boolean; uncommon: boolean }
48	export function rollRarities(L: number): DropRarity {
49	  const one = (num: number, min: number) => Math.random() < 1 / Math.max(Math.floor(num / Math.max(1, L)), min);
50	  return {
51	    legendary: one(4500, 6),   // :20134/20160
52	    veryrare: one(2250, 5),    // :20130/20156
53	    rare: one(1050, 4),        // :20126/20152
54	    uncommon: one(300, 3),     // :20122/20148
55	  };
56	}
57	
58	/** 掷一次渔获（FishingCheck 规则表序：Junk→Crate→RareDrops→普通池；
59	 *  GameContentFishDropPopulator 注册序 :12-30，第一条命中即返回）。
60	 *  power = 钓鱼力（含乘区）；ctx = 血月/战斗书旗标（4382 门 :19650-19654） */
61	export function rollCatch(
62	  power: number, pool: FishPool, cratePotion: boolean,
63	  ctx?: { rarity: DropRarity; bloodMoon: boolean; combatBook: boolean },
64	): number {
65	  const L = Math.max(1, Math.floor(power));
66	  // junk（原版 :19326 双 Next(50) 门——水量未跟踪，用低力近似公式）
67	  const junkChance = Math.max(0.05, 0.35 - power * 0.004);
68	  if (pool.junk?.length && Math.random() < junkChance) {
69	    return pool.junk[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.junk.length)];
70	  }
71	  // crate（:20113-20117：10%，宝匣药水 +15）
72	  if (pool.crates?.length && Math.random() < 0.1 + (cratePotion ? 0.15 : 0)) {
73	    return pool.crates[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.crates.length)];
74	  }
75	  // RareDrops 传说族（:196-201，串行独立 roll 各自失败才落下一条）：
76	  //   血月 4382（须 !combatBookWasUsed）→ 血月 5240 → 2423/3225/2420
77	  if (ctx?.rarity) {
78	    if (ctx.rarity.legendary && ctx.bloodMoon && !ctx.combatBook && Math.random() < 1 / 3) return 4382; // Next(3)==0（:19650-19654）
79	    if (ctx.rarity.legendary && ctx.bloodMoon && Math.random() < 0.5) return 5240;
80	    if (ctx.rarity.legendary && Math.random() < 0.2) return 2423;
81	    if (ctx.rarity.legendary && Math.random() < 0.2) return 3225;
82	    if (ctx.rarity.legendary && Math.random() < 0.1) return 2420;
83	  }
84	  return pool.fish[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.fish.length)];
85	}
86	
87	/** Fishing_GetPowerMultiplier（Player.cs:41560-41605 1:1）：天气/时段/月相/血月乘区。
88	 *  time = 当时段 tick（昼 0-54000 / 夜 0-32400）。 */
89	export function fishingPowerMultiplier(ctx: {
90	  raining: boolean; cloudBGAlpha: number; dayTime: boolean; time: number;
91	  moonPhase: number; bloodMoon: boolean;
92	}): number {
93	  let m = 1;
94	  if (ctx.raining) m *= 1.2;
95	  if (ctx.cloudBGAlpha > 0) m *= 1.1;
96	  if (ctx.dayTime && (ctx.time < 5400 || ctx.time > 48600)) m *= 1.3;          // 晨昏
97	  if (ctx.dayTime && ctx.time > 16200 && ctx.time < 37800) m *= 0.8;           // 正午
98	  if (!ctx.dayTime && ctx.time > 6480 && ctx.time < 25920) m *= 0.8;           // 前半夜
99	  if (ctx.moonPhase === 0) m *= 1.1;                                           // 满月
100	  if (ctx.moonPhase === 1 || ctx.moonPhase === 7) m *= 1.05;
101	  if (ctx.moonPhase === 3 || ctx.moonPhase === 5) m *= 0.95;
102	  if (ctx.moonPhase === 4) m *= 0.9;                                           // 新月
103	  if (ctx.bloodMoon) m *= 1.1;
104	  return m;
105	}
106
```

```
33	function getFishingPondState(game: GameHooks, x: number, y: number): PondState {

39	  while (maxX < st.w - 10 && liquidAt(maxX, y) > 0 && !st.isSolid(maxX, y)) maxX++;
40	  let lava = false, honey = false, numWaters = 0;             // :20174-20177
41	  for (let i = minX; i <= maxX; i++) {                        // :20180-20194
42	    for (let j = y; st.inBounds(i, j) && liquidAt(i, j) > 0 && !st.isSolid(i, j) && j < st.h - 10; j++) {
43	      numWaters++;
44	      const lt = st.liquidType[st.idx(i, j)];
45	      if (lt === 2) lava = true;                              // tile.lava()
46	      else if (lt === 3) honey = true;                        // tile.honey()
47	    }
48	  }
49	  if (honey) numWaters = Math.floor(numWaters * 1.5);         // :20197-20200
50	  return { lava, honey, waterTilesCount: numWaters };
51	}
52	
53	export class Bobber extends Entity {
54	  w = 8; h = 8;
55	  dead = false;
56	  /** 漂浮中（已落液） */
57	  floating = false;
58	  /** 咬钩窗口剩余 tick（>0 = 咬钩中，可收竿）。原版 ai[1] 的正值镜像
59	   *  （AI_061 :50918-50934：负值每 tick += rand(1,5)，归零 = 逃脱） */
60	  get biteT(): number { return this.ai1 < 0 ? Math.ceil(-this.ai1) : 0; }
61	  /** 咬钩时的渔获（声呐药水预掷展示） */
62	  catchId = 0;
63	  /** 血月咬钩敌怪（FishingCheck_RollEnemySpawns）——收竿由 Game 侧生成 */
64	  catchEnemy = 0;
65	  /** 渔获原版 id → 钓起时落物用 */
66	  private polePower: number;
67	  poleVid = 0;
68	  private baitSlot: number;
69	  private baitPower: number;
70	  private ownerRef: () => Player | null;
71	  private bobPhase = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
72	  private airT = 0;
73	  /** ai[1]（AI_061）：0 = 等待累积中；<0 = 咬钩窗口（负值幅度 = rand(-240,-90) - 钓鱼力） */
74	  private ai1 = 0;
75	  /** localAI[1]：等待累积器（>660 触发 FishingCheck，:50912-50915） */
76	  private lAI1 = 0;
77	
78	  constructor(
79	    x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number,
80	    polePower: number, baitSlot: number, baitPower: number,
81	    owner: () => Player | null,
82	  ) {
83	    super();
84	    this.x = x; this.y = y; this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
85	    this.polePower = polePower;
86	    this.baitSlot = baitSlot;
87	    this.baitPower = baitPower;
88	    this.ownerRef = owner;
89	  }
90	
91	  /** 钓鱼力（竿+饵+药水 121 +15；声呐 122/宝匣 123 由收竿侧消费）。
92	   *  game 传入时追加 Fishing_GetPowerMultiplier 乘区（Player.cs:41560-41605：
93	   *  雨/云/晨昏/正午/前半夜/月相/血月） */
94	  private fishingPower(p: Player, game?: GameHooks): number {
95	    // buff 121 钓鱼药水 +15（Player.cs:9831 fishingSkill += 15）
96	    let lvl = this.polePower + this.baitPower + (p.buffs.has(BuffType.Fishing) ? 15 : 0);
97	    // 幸运度修正（Projectile.cs:19267-19276 FishingAttempt 主干，1:1）：
98	    //   luck<0 且 rand<|luck| → ×(0.9 - rand*0.3)（0.6-0.9 倍）
99	    //   luck>0 且 rand<luck  → ×(1.1 + rand*0.3)（1.1-1.4 倍）
100	    const luck = p.luck;
101	    if (luck < 0) {
102	      if (Math.random() < -luck) lvl = Math.floor(lvl * (0.9 - Math.random() * 0.3));
103	    } else if (Math.random() < luck) {
104	      lvl = Math.floor(lvl * (1.1 + Math.random() * 0.3));
105	    }
106	    // Fishing_GetPowerMultiplier（:41560-41605）：天气/时段/月相/血月乘区
107	    if (game) {
108	      const w = game.world as World;
109	      const t = w.clock.timeOfDay;
110	      const isDay = w.clock.isDay; // 昼夜边界以 Clock 为准（4:30/19:30）
111	      lvl = Math.floor(lvl * fishingPowerMultiplier({
112	        raining: !!w.weather?.raining,
113	        cloudBGAlpha: w.weather?.cloudAlpha ?? 0,
114	        dayTime: isDay,
115	        time: isDay ? ((t - 0.25) / 0.5) * 54000 : ((((t - 0.75) + 1) % 1) / 0.5) * 32400,
116	        moonPhase: w.clock.moonPhase,
117	        bloodMoon: !!w.clock.bloodMoon,
118	      }));
119	    }
120	    return lvl;
121	  }
122	
123	  fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
124	    void dt;
125	    const p = this.ownerRef();
126	    if (!p || p.dead) { this.dead = true; return; }
127	    const st = (game.world as World).store;
128	    const idx = st.idx(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor((this.y + this.h) / TILE));
129	    const liq = st.liquid[idx];
130	    const liqType: LiquidType | null = liq > 60 ? ((st.liquidType[idx] === 2 ? 'lava' : st.liquidType[idx] === 3 ? 'honey' : 'water')) : null;
131	
132	    if (!this.floating) {
133	      this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY * 0.7, 8);
134	      this.vx *= 0.99;
135	      moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
136	      if (liqType === 'water' || liqType === 'honey') {
137	        this.floating = true;
138	        this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;
139	        // 原版浮标经 AI_061_GetWaterLine（:50947-50978）沉到液面以下、中心格 wet；
140	        // 此处把中心对齐到入液行中部，保证 FishingCheck 的 Center/16 坐标落在液格内
141	        const rowY = Math.floor((this.y + this.h) / TILE);
142	        this.y = rowY * TILE + (TILE - this.h) / 2;
143	        game.playSfx('splash', 0.6);
144	      } else if (liqType === 'lava') {
145	        // 岩浆：无熔线钓钩配饰则浮标烧毁（accLavaFishing 门）
146	        const canLava = this.ownerLavaOk();
147	        if (!canLava) { this.dead = true; return; }
148	        this.floating = true;
149	        this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;
150	        const rowYL = Math.floor((this.y + this.h) / TILE);
151	        this.y = rowYL * TILE + (TILE - this.h) / 2;
152	      } else if (++this.airT > 600) {
153	        this.dead = true; // 落地未入水超时
154	      }
155	      return;
156	    }
157	    // 漂浮：水面起伏
158	    this.bobPhase += 0.15;
159	    if (this.ai1 < 0) {
160	      // 咬钩窗口（AI_061 :50918-50934）：ai[1] 每 tick += rand(1,5)；
161	      // 归零 = 逃脱——localAI[1] 清零（渔获作废），回到等待累积
162	      this.ai1 += nextInt(1, 5);
163	      if (this.ai1 >= 0) {
164	        this.ai1 = 0;        // :50930
165	        this.lAI1 = 0;       // :50931（渔获作废）
166	        this.catchId = 0;
167	        this.catchEnemy = 0;
168	      }
169	      return;
170	    }
171	    // 等待累积（AI_061 :50897-50915）
172	    const level = this.fishingPower(p, game);
173	    if (Math.floor(Math.random() * 300) < level) this.lAI1 += nextInt(1, 3);   // :50900-50902
174	    this.lAI1 += Math.floor(level / 30);                                       // :50903
175	    this.lAI1 += nextInt(1, 3);                                                // :50904
176	    if (Math.floor(Math.random() * 60) === 0) this.lAI1 += 60;                 // :50905-50908
177	    if (this.lAI1 > 660) {
178	      this.lAI1 = 0;                                                           // :50912
179	      this.fishingCheck(p, game, idx);
180	    }
181	  }
182	
183	  /** FishingCheck（Projectile.cs:19175-19185）+ TryBuildFishingContext 水体段
184	   *  （:19188-19249）：咬钩门 rand(100) <= ⌊(钓鱼力+75)/2⌋；水体 <75 直接不咬。 */
185	  private fishingCheck(p: Player, game: GameHooks, idx: number): void {
186	    const w = game.world as World;
187	    const st = w.store;
188	    const tx = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), ty = Math.floor(this.cy / TILE);
189	    const pond = getFishingPondState(game, tx, ty);   // GetFishingPondState :20170-20201
190	    if (pond.waterTilesCount < 75) return;            // :19208-19211 NotEnoughWater
191	    let level = this.fishingPower(p, game);           // FinalFishingLevel（Player.cs:41536-41557）
192	    // 水量缩放（:19234-19249）：atmo = ⌊(Y/16 - (60 + 10·(W/4200)²)) / (地表/6)⌋ 截 [0.25,1]
193	    const atmoNum = (st.w / 4200) ** 2;
194	    let atmo = (this.y / TILE - (60 + 10 * atmoNum)) / (w.groundLevel / 6);
195	    if (atmo < 0.25) atmo = 0.25;
196	    if (atmo > 1) atmo = 1;
197	    const waterNeeded = Math.floor(300 * atmo);       // waterNeededToFish = 300 × atmo
198	    if (waterNeeded > 0) {
199	      const quality = pond.waterTilesCount / waterNeeded;
200	      if (quality < 1) level = Math.floor(level * quality);   // :19244-19246
201	    }
202	    // 咬钩门（:19179-19184）：Next(100) <= ⌊(力+75)/2⌋ 才真咬（否则空拍再等下一轮）
203	    const num = Math.floor((level + 75) / 2);
204	    if (Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) > num) return;
205	    // 血月钓鱼敌怪（FishingCheck_RollEnemySpawns :19399-19427，SetFishingCheckResults
206	    // 内先敌怪后渔获）：水中、血月夜、1/6（血肉抛竿 4325 → 1/3）→ 咬的是敌怪：
207	    // 困难 {620,621,586,587}+1/10 恐惧鹦鹉螺 618；非困难 {586,587}。
208	    this.catchEnemy = 0;
209	    const inWater = st.liquidType[idx] !== 2 && st.liquidType[idx] !== 3;
210	    if (inWater && w.clock.bloodMoon && !w.clock.isDay) {
211	      const chance = this.poleVid === 4325 ? 3 : 6;
212	      if (Math.random() < 1 / chance) {
213	        // 红色城镇史莱姆首获（:19413-19416：世界级一次性旗标，1/5 优先于敌怪表；
214	        //  收竿侧置 unlockedSlimeRedSpawn）
215	        if (!w.flags.unlockedSlimeRedSpawn && Math.random() < 0.2) {
216	          this.catchEnemy = 682;
217	        } else {
218	          const hard = w.flags.hardMode;
219	          const poolE = hard ? [620, 621, 586, 587] : [586, 587];
220	          this.catchEnemy = hard && Math.random() < 0.1 ? 618
221	            : poolE[Math.floor(Math.random() * poolE.length)];
222	        }
223	        this.openBiteWindow(level);
224	        game.playSfx('splash', 0.8);
225	        game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#C04040', 6, 1.5, { life: 24 });
226	        return;
227	      }
228	    }
229	    const pool = pickPool({
230	      liquid: (st.liquidType[idx] === 2 ? 'lava' : st.liquidType[idx] === 3 ? 'honey' : 'water'),
231	      zone: this.zoneOf(game),
232	      depth: this.depthOf(game),
233	      zoneOcean: this.zoneOcean(game, pond.waterTilesCount),
234	    });
235	    this.catchId = rollCatch(level, pool, p.buffs.has(BuffType.Crate), {
236	      rarity: rollRarities(level),
237	      bloodMoon: !!w.clock.bloodMoon,
238	      combatBook: !!w.flags.combatBookWasUsed,
239	    });
240	    this.openBiteWindow(level);
241	    game.playSfx('splash', 0.8);
242	    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#80B8FF', 6, 1.5, { life: 24 });
243	  }
244	
245	  /** 开咬钩窗口（SetFishingCheckResults :19334 / :19344）：
246	   *  ai[1] = rand(-240,-90) - 钓鱼力（负值，窗口长短随钓鱼力递增） */
247	  private openBiteWindow(level: number): void {
248	    this.ai1 = nextInt(-240, -90) - level;
249	  }
250	
251	  /** 收竿（窗口内）：返回渔获原版 id（0=没咬/没饵/血月敌怪——后者查 catchEnemy）；消耗鱼饵 */
252	  reel(game: GameHooks): number {
253	    if (this.biteT <= 0 || (!this.catchId && !this.catchEnemy)) return 0;
254	    const p = this.ownerRef();
255	    if (!p) return 0;
256	    // 声呐药水（buff 122）：咬钩时已掷——由 Game 侧显示（本实体的 catchId 预存）
257	    this.consumeBait(p);
258	    const caught = this.catchId;
259	    this.catchId = 0;
260	    this.dead = true;
261	    void game;
262	    return caught;
263	  }
264	
265	  /** 鱼饵消耗（:51640：1/(1+bait/6)，钓具箱 +1 分母） */
266	  private consumeBait(p: Player): boolean {
267	    const slot = p.inv.slots[this.baitSlot];
268	    if (!slot) return false;
269	    const chance = 1 / (1 + this.baitPower / 6 + (this.tackleBox(p) ? 1 : 0));
270	    if (Math.random() < chance) p.inv.removeAt(this.baitSlot, 1);
271	    return true;
272	  }
273	
274	  private tackleBox(p: Player): boolean {
275	    // 钓具箱族（accfx 未提取该字段——经装备名快速判定，渔夫耳环 2374/渔具袋 3721/钓具箱 2375）
276	    return (p as unknown as { equipStats: { fishingTackle?: boolean } }).equipStats?.fishingTackle ?? false;
277	  }
278	
279	  private ownerLavaOk(): boolean {
280	    const p = this.ownerRef();
281	    if (!p) return false;
282	    return (p as unknown as { equipStats: { lavaFishing?: boolean } }).equipStats?.lavaFishing ?? false;
283	  }
284	
285	  private zoneOf(game: GameHooks): string {
286	    const w = game.world as World;
287	    const x = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE);
288	    const y = Math.floor(this.cy / TILE);
289	    const st = w.store;
290	    // 按 tile key 采样（玩家 zone 通道未接此处，浮标位置独立判定）
291	    for (let dy = -2; dy <= 0; dy++) {
292	      const t = st.get(x, y + dy);
293	      const key = t ? TILE_DEFS[t]?.key ?? '' : '';
294	      if (!key) continue;
295	      if (/corrupt/.test(key)) return 'corrupt';
296	      if (/crimson/.test(key)) return 'crimson';
297	      if (/hallow/.test(key)) return 'hallow';
298	      if (/jungle/.test(key)) return 'jungle';
299	      if (/snow/.test(key)) return 'snow';
300	    }
301	    return 'forest';
302	  }
303	
304	  private depthOf(game: GameHooks): 'sky' | 'surface' | 'cavern' | 'underworld' {
305	    const w = game.world as World;
306	    const y = Math.floor(this.cy / TILE);
307	    if (y < Math.floor(w.groundLevel * 0.35)) return 'sky';
308	    if (y <= w.groundLevel + 10) return 'surface';
309	    if (y > (w.lavaLine || w.store.h - 200)) return 'underworld';
310	    return 'cavern';
311	  }
312	
313	  /** 海洋判定（FishingCheck_RollItemDropOld :19886）：heightLevel <= 1（浮标在地表以上，
314	   *  即 Y < worldSurface）&& 横向边缘 380 格内 && 该处水体 >1000 格——三者同时成立。 */
315	  private zoneOcean(game: GameHooks, waterTilesCount: number): boolean {
316	    const w = game.world as World;
317	    const x = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE);
318	    const y = Math.floor(this.cy / TILE);
319	    return y < w.groundLevel && (x < 380 || x > w.store.w - 380) && waterTilesCount > 1000;
320	  }
321	
322	  /** 声呐预展示的渔获名（Game 侧在浮标上方画字） */
323	  sonarLabel(): string | null {
324	    if (this.biteT > 0 && this.catchId) {
325	      const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[this.catchId];
326	      return key ? key : null;
327	    }
328	    return null;
329	  }
330	
331	  draw(r: import('../render/Renderer').Renderer): void {
332	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
333	    if (!ctx) return;
334	    ctx.save();
335	    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
336	    ctx.fillStyle = this.biteT > 0 ? '#FF6060' : '#E04838';
337	    const dip = this.biteT > 0 ? 2 : Math.sin(this.bobPhase) * 1.5;
338	    ctx.beginPath();
339	    ctx.arc(0, dip, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
340	    ctx.fill();
341	    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
342	    ctx.fillRect(-1, dip - 6, 2, 4); // 竿线连杆近似
343	    ctx.restore();
344	  }
345	}
346
```

```
1	using System.Linq;
2	
3	namespace Terraria.GameContent.FishDropRules;
4	
5	public abstract class AFishDropRulePopulator
6	{
7		private class DelegateFishingCondition : AFishingCondition
8		{
9			public delegate bool MatchCondition(FishingContext context);
10	
11			private MatchCondition _condition;
12	
13			public DelegateFishingCondition(MatchCondition innerCondition)
14			{
15				_condition = innerCondition;
16			}
17	
18			public override bool Matches(FishingContext context)
19			{
20				return _condition(context);
21			}
22		}
23
```

```
20195			}

20198				numWaters = (int)((double)numWaters * 1.5);
20199			}
20200		}
```

```
3	public class FishDropRule
4	{
5		public int[] PossibleItems;
6	
7		public int ChanceNumerator = 1;
8	
9		public int ChanceDenominator = 1;
10	
11		public AFishingCondition[] Conditions;
12	
13		public FishRarityCondition Rarity;
14	
15		public bool IsStopper
16		{
17			get
18			{
19				if (PossibleItems.Length != 0)
20				{
21					if (Rarity.HackedIsAny)
22					{
23						return ChanceDenominator == ChanceNumerator;
24					}
25					return false;
26				}
27				return true;
28			}
29		}
30	
31		public bool Attempt(FishingContext context, out int resultItemType)
32		{
33			resultItemType = 0;
34			if (!MeetsConditions(context, forDisplay: false))
35			{
36				return false;
37			}
38			if (context.Random.Next(ChanceDenominator) >= ChanceNumerator)
39			{
40				return false;
41			}
42			if (!Rarity.Matches(context))
43			{
44				return false;
45			}
46			if (PossibleItems != null && PossibleItems.Length != 0)
47			{
48				resultItemType = context.Random.NextFromList(PossibleItems);
49			}
50			return true;
51		}
52	
53		public bool MeetsConditions(FishingContext context, bool forDisplay)
54		{
55			AFishingCondition[] conditions = Conditions;
56			for (int i = 0; i < conditions.Length; i++)
57			{
58				if (!conditions[i].Matches(context))
59				{
60					return false;
61				}
62			}
63			return true;
64		}
65	}
```

```
49	    this.timeOfDay += dtMs / this.dayLengthMs;

198	  /** 最近一次 markExplored 新点亮格的包围盒（tile 坐标；null = 无新探索）。
199	   *  渲染端雾画布按此做脏矩形增量更新——旧版无条件 bump 版本导致每 15 tick
200	   *  整幅重建 4200×1200 雾画布（20MB 分配 + 500 万格循环 ≈ 672ms 长任务），
201	   *  首次导入大世界开地图时主线程持续阻塞 → 白屏闪烁 + 标签页 OOM 崩溃 */
202	  exploredDirty: { x0: number; y0: number; x1: number; y1: number } | null = null;
203	  markExplored(cx: number, cy: number, radius: number) {
204	    const st = this.store;
205	    const x0 = Math.max(0, cx - radius), x1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, cx + radius);
206	    const y0 = Math.max(0, cy - radius), y1 = Math.min(st.h - 1, cy + radius);
207	    let changed = false;
208	    for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {
209	      for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
210	        const i = y * st.w + x;
211	        if (!this.explored[i] && (x - cx) ** 2 + (y - cy) ** 2 <= radius * radius) {
212	          this.explored[i] = 1;
213	          changed = true;
214	          // 脏包围盒与新点亮格取并（渲染帧间多次 mark 不丢更新）
215	          const d = this.exploredDirty;
216	          if (d) { if (x < d.x0) d.x0 = x; if (x > d.x1) d.x1 = x; if (y < d.y0) d.y0 = y; if (y > d.y1) d.y1 = y; }
217	          else this.exploredDirty = { x0: x, y0: y, x1: x, y1: y };
218	        }
219	      }
220	    }
221	    if (changed) this.exploredVersion++;
222	  }
223	
224	  constructor(w: number, h: number, seed: number, name = '新世界') {
225	    this.store = new TileStore(w, h);
226	    this.explored = new Uint8Array(w * h);
227	    this.seed = seed;
228	    this.name = name;
229	    // 1 游戏日 = 30 现实分钟（24→40 后折中）
230	    this.clock = new Clock(24 * 60 * 1000); // 1:1 全周期 24min（昼 15/夜 9）
231	  }
232	
233	  /** 从 worker 数据包重建（buffer 已 transfer 移交，零拷贝包装）。
234	   *  explored 未包含在包内（saveGame 不持久化、新生成/读档均全零）时按全零分配 */
235	  static fromPacket(p: import('../workers/protocol').WorldPacket): World {
236	    const w = new World(p.w, p.h, p.seed, p.name);
237	    w.store = new TileStore(p.w, p.h, p.buf);
238	    w.explored = p.buf.explored ? new Uint8Array(p.buf.explored) : new Uint8Array(p.w * p.h);
239	    w.spawnX = p.spawnX; w.spawnY = p.spawnY;
240	    w.groundLevel = p.groundLevel; w.rockLevel = p.rockLevel; w.lavaLine = p.lavaLine;
241	    w.crimson = p.crimson; w.dungeonX = p.dungeonX; w.dungeonY = p.dungeonY; w.jungleX = p.jungleX;
242	    w.exploredVersion = p.exploredVersion;
243	    if (p.difficulty !== undefined) w.difficulty = p.difficulty;   // Main.GameMode（旧包缺省 0）
244	    w.clock.timeOfDay = p.clock.timeOfDay;
245	    w.clock.dayCount = p.clock.dayCount;
246	    if (p.clock.bloodMoon !== undefined) w.clock.bloodMoon = p.clock.bloodMoon;
247	    if (p.clock.eclipse !== undefined) w.clock.eclipse = p.clock.eclipse;
248	    if (p.clock.moonPhase !== undefined) w.clock.moonPhase = p.clock.moonPhase;
249	    if (p.seedFlags) w.seedFlags = { ...p.seedFlags };
250	    if (p.moonType !== undefined) w.moonType = p.moonType;
251	    if (p.invasion) { w.invasionType = p.invasion.type; w.invasionSize = p.invasion.size;
252	      w.invasionSizeStart = p.invasion.sizeStart; w.invasionX = p.invasion.x; w.invasionDelay = p.invasion.delay; }
253	    if (p.altarCount !== undefined) w.altarCount = p.altarCount;
254	    // 日晷/月晷四件套（WorldFile.cs:1311/1342/1416-1417；旧包缺省=无快进无冷却）
255	    if (p.sundial !== undefined) {
256	      w.sundialCooldown = p.sundial;
257	      w.moondialCooldown = p.moondial ?? 0;
258	      w.fastForwardTimeToDawn = p.fastForwardTimeToDawn ?? false;
259	      w.fastForwardTimeToDusk = p.fastForwardTimeToDusk ?? false;
260	    }
261	    if (p.weather) w.applyWeather(p.weather);
262	    if (p.bestiary) w.applyBestiary(p.bestiary);
263	    w.pendingTownNpcs = p.pendingTownNpcs;
264	    w.chests = p.chests;
265	    w.signs = p.signs;
266	    w.furnitureItems = p.furnitureItems ?? [];
267	    w.trees = p.trees;
268	    w.flags = p.flags;
269	    w.treeX = p.treeX; w.treeStyle = p.treeStyle; w.treeTops = p.treeTops;
270	    w.shimmerX = p.shimmerX ?? 0; w.shimmerY = p.shimmerY ?? 0;
271	    return w;
272	  }
273	
274	  get w() { return this.store.w; }
275	  get h() { return this.store.h; }
276	  get tiles() { return this.store; }
277	
278	  /** 破坏一个 tile（含 framed 物体的锚点处理由调用方负责），返回掉落 item key */
279	  breakTileAt(x: number, y: number): string | null {
280	    const t = this.store.get(x, y);
281	    if (t === 0) return null;
282	    const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
283	    this.store.setTile(x, y, 0);
284	    // v_ 图块无显式 drop → 回退同名放置物品(阶段5 自动注册)。
285	    // drop:null = 显式无掉落(原版装饰类)——?? 会把 null 也走兜底,必须显式判 undefined。
286	    // 数字 drop = 原版 item id(本地物品退役后统一),经 VI() 落到 vi_ 键
287	    if (typeof d.drop === 'number') return VI(d.drop);
288	    return d.drop === undefined ? (d.key.startsWith('v_') ? 'place_' + d.key : null) : d.drop;
289	  }
290	
291	  isSolidPx(px: number, py: number): boolean {
292	    return this.store.isSolid(Math.floor(px / 16), Math.floor(py / 16));
293	  }
294	
295	  /** 液体量（0-255）按像素坐标 */
296	  liquidAt(px: number, py: number): number {
297	    return this.store.liquid[this.store.idx(Math.floor(px / 16), Math.floor(py / 16))];
298	  }
299	
300	  registerTree(x: number, y: number, h: number) {
301	    this.trees.push({ x, y, h });
302	  }
303	}
```

```
  hasSunflower: boolean;
```

```
.sw-shop-item .nm { flex: 1; }
.sw-shop-item .pr { color: #ffd76e; }
.sw-shop-foot { display: flex; justify-content: flex-end; margin-top: 10px; }
`;

/** 铜币计价格式化(原版 tooltip:金/银/铜) */
export function formatCopper(v: number): string {
  const gold = Math.floor(v / 10000);
  const silver = Math.floor((v % 10000) / 100);
  const copper = v % 100;
  let s = '';
  if (gold) s += `${gold}金`;
  if (silver) s += `${silver}银`;
  if (copper || !s) s += `${copper}铜`;
```

```
15	  private grow(need: number) {
16	    if (this.len + need <= this.buf.length) return;
17	    let cap = this.buf.length;
18	    while (cap < this.len + need) cap *= 2;
19	    const nb = new Uint8Array(cap);

25	    this.grow(5);  // 32 位 varint 至多 5 字节
26	    while (v >= 0x80) {
27	      this.buf[this.len++] = (v & 0x7f) | 0x80;
28	      v >>>= 7;
29	    }

37	export function bytesToB64(u8: Uint8Array): string {
38	  // 分块拼接(8192)避免逐字符 += 的 ConsString 链;输出与逐字符版一致

114	     *  Main.buffNoSave 已过滤——旧档缺省=无 buff） */
115	    buffs?: Array<[number, number]>;
116	    /** 税务员累积税款（Player.taxMoney 铜币,PL:53839;旧档缺省 0） */
117	    taxMoney?: number;

123	  npcs: Array<{ key: string; x: number; y: number; home: [number, number] | null; bound?: boolean; givenName?: string }>;
124	  signs?: Array<{ x: number; y: number; text: string }>; // 墓碑碑文（旧档缺省无）
125	  /** 世界事件态（原版 header 内 party/灯笼夜段,WF:1361-1368/1381-1384;旧档缺省=无派对无预约） */
126	  events?: {
127	    partyManual: boolean;            // BirthdayParty.ManualParty
128	    lanternManual: boolean;          // 服务端手动灯笼（本仓无入口,位保留）
129	    lanternGenuine: boolean;         // 真灯笼夜兑现中
130	    lanternNext: boolean;            // 次夜灯笼夜预约
131	    lanternCooldown: number;         // 冷却夜数
132	  };
133	  /** 挂物族存放物（物品框 395/武器架 471/模特 470/帽架 475/食物盘 520/展示罐 698；
134	   *  原版 TileEntity 段的最小等价，旧档缺省无） */
135	  furniture?: FurnitureSaveEntry[];
136	  flags: Record<string, boolean>;
137	  weather?: WeatherSave; // 天气快照（雨/风/云/沙尘暴；旧档缺省=晴）
138	  /** 图鉴统计（BestiaryUnlocksTracker——原版随世界文件持久化 WorldFile.cs:3399/3405；
139	   *  旧档缺省=空三桶） */
140	  bestiary?: import('../data/Bestiary').BestiarySave;
141	}
142	
143	/** 存档物品实例（Inventory.InvItem 的可序列化形——favorited/prefix 为实例态可选字段） */
144	export interface SavedItem {
145	  id: number; stack: number;
146	  favorited?: boolean;
147	  prefix?: number; // 重铸词缀（Item.prefix；0/缺省=无）
148	}
149	
150	/** 序列化所需的 tilestore 视图(worker 路径=副本包装;主线程=活存储) */
151	export interface SaveStoreView {
152	  type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array;
153	  frameX: Uint16Array; frameY: Uint16Array;
154	  wall: Uint16Array; liquid: Uint8Array; liquidType: Uint8Array; wire: Uint8Array;
155	  half: Uint8Array; slope: Uint8Array;
156	}
157	/** 序列化所需的世界元数据(全部可结构化克隆) */
158	export interface SaveMeta {
159	  name: string; seed: number; w: number; h: number;
160	  spawnX: number; spawnY: number; groundLevel: number; rockLevel: number; lavaLine?: number;
161	  treeX?: number[]; treeStyle?: number[]; treeTops?: number[];
162	  crimson?: boolean; dungeonX?: number; dungeonY?: number; jungleX?: number;
163	  timeOfDay: number; dayCount: number;
164	  bloodMoon?: boolean; eclipse?: boolean; moonPhase?: number; moonType?: number;
165	  seedFlags?: Record<string, boolean>;
166	  /** 世界难度 = Main.GameMode（Main.cs:2677；旧档缺省 0=经典） */
167	  difficulty?: number;
168	  /** 旅程时间倍率/冻结（CreativePowers.cs:866/:1448；旧档缺省 1/false） */
169	  journeyTimeRate?: number;
170	  journeyFreezeTime?: boolean;
171	  /** 日晷/月晷快进四件套（WorldFile.cs:1311/1342/1416-1417；旧档缺省 0/0/false/false） */
172	  sundialCooldown?: number; moondialCooldown?: number;
173	  fastForwardTimeToDawn?: boolean; fastForwardTimeToDusk?: boolean;
174	  chests: SaveData['chests']; signs?: SaveData['signs']; furniture?: FurnitureSaveEntry[]; flags: Record<string, boolean>;
175	  weather?: WeatherSave;
176	  invasion?: { type: number; size: number; sizeStart: number; x: number; delay: number };
177	  /** 已砸祭坛数（WorldGen.altarCount，WorldFile.cs:1303/2100；旧档缺省 0） */
178	  altarCount?: number;
179	  /** 图鉴三桶（杀数/遇数/聊天）——data/Bestiary.ts BestiarySave */
180	  bestiary?: import('../data/Bestiary').BestiarySave;
181	}
182	
183	export function rlePairs(values: ArrayLike<number>): Uint8Array {
184	  const out = new ByteWriter();
185	  const n = values.length;
186	  let i = 0;
187	  while (i < n) {
188	    const v = values[i];
189	    let run = 1;
190	    while (i + run < n && values[i + run] === v && run < 0x7fffffff) run++;
191	    out.varint(run - 1);
192	    out.varint(v);
193	    i += run;
194	  }
195	  return out.bytes();
196	}
197	
198	/** RLE tiles。stableMap 非空时逐元素映射为稳定 id(v3 存档路径) */
199	export function rleTiles(st: { type: Uint16Array; frameX: Uint16Array; frameY: Uint16Array }, stableMap?: Uint16Array): Uint8Array {
200	  const out = new ByteWriter();
201	  const n = st.type.length;
202	  let i = 0;
203	  while (i < n) {
204	    const raw = st.type[i];
205	    const t = stableMap ? (raw === 0 ? 0 : stableMap[raw]) : raw;
206	    const fx = st.frameX[i], fy = st.frameY[i];
207	    let run = 1;
208	    // 游程比较必须 raw 对 raw（t 是映射后的稳定 id，与原始 id 比较恒不等 →
209	    // 自定义 tile（stable≠internal）游程全断，4200x1200 规则地形 17B 膨胀到 21MB）
210	    while (i + run < n && st.type[i + run] === raw && st.frameX[i + run] === fx && st.frameY[i + run] === fy) run++;
211	    out.varint(run - 1);
212	    out.varint(t);
213	    if (t !== 0) {
214	      out.varint(fx);
215	      out.varint(fy);
216	    }
217	    i += run;
218	  }
219	  return out.bytes();
220	}
221	
222	/** 存档序列化主体。幽灵净化在视图上就地执行(主线程=活存储原语义;
223	 * worker=副本,净化不影响游戏内状态,存档字节一致) */
224	export function serializeSave(
225	  st: SaveStoreView, meta: SaveMeta,
226	  player: SaveData['player'], playTimeMs: number,
227	  townNpcs?: SaveData['npcs'],
228	  events?: SaveData['events'],
229	): string {
230	  // 幽灵净化:存档格式不携带 active 位(加载时 type≠0 即激活),
231	  // 非活性格上的残留 type 必须归零,否则重读后全部变成浮空实体块
232	  for (let i = 0; i < st.type.length; i++) {
233	    if (!st.flags[i] && st.type[i] !== 0) st.type[i] = 0;
234	  }
235	  // 物品 id → 稳定 id(全槽位统一;量小直接遍历)。保留 favorited/prefix。
236	  // v4 起 armor/dye/trash/miscEquips/miscDyes/loadouts 也走稳定 id(v3 曾裸透传内部
237	  // 下标——注册表一变即错位;本地物品退役时顺手根治)
238	  const mapItem = (it: { id: number; stack: number; favorited?: boolean; prefix?: number } | null) =>
239	    it
240	      ? {
241	        id: it.id === 0 ? 0 : ITEM_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL[it.id] ?? 0,
242	        stack: it.stack,
243	        favorited: it.favorited || undefined,
244	        prefix: it.prefix || undefined,
245	      }
246	      : null;
247	  const data: SaveData = {
248	    format: 'sandboxworld.save',
249	    version: 4,
250	    header: {
251	      name: meta.name, seed: meta.seed, width: meta.w, height: meta.h,
252	      spawn: [meta.spawnX, meta.spawnY], groundLevel: meta.groundLevel, rockLevel: meta.rockLevel,
253	      lavaLine: meta.lavaLine,
254	      treeX: meta.treeX, treeStyle: meta.treeStyle, treeTops: meta.treeTops,
255	      crimson: meta.crimson, dungeonX: meta.dungeonX, dungeonY: meta.dungeonY, jungleX: meta.jungleX,
256	      created: Date.now(), playTimeMs,
257	      moonType: meta.moonType ?? 0,
258	      seedFlags: meta.seedFlags,
259	      difficulty: meta.difficulty ?? 0,   // Main.GameMode（旧档缺省=经典）
260	      journeyTimeRate: meta.journeyTimeRate ?? 1,
261	      journeyFreezeTime: meta.journeyFreezeTime ?? false,
262	      sundialCooldown: meta.sundialCooldown ?? 0,
263	      moondialCooldown: meta.moondialCooldown ?? 0,
264	      fastForwardTimeToDawn: meta.fastForwardTimeToDawn ?? false,
265	      fastForwardTimeToDusk: meta.fastForwardTimeToDusk ?? false,
266	    },
267	    clock: {
268	      timeOfDay: meta.timeOfDay, dayCount: meta.dayCount,
269	      bloodMoon: meta.bloodMoon ?? false, eclipse: meta.eclipse ?? false,
270	      moonPhase: meta.moonPhase ?? 0,
271	    },
272	    invasion: meta.invasion ?? { type: 0, size: 0, sizeStart: 0, x: 0, delay: 0 },
273	    altarCount: meta.altarCount ?? 0,
274	    tiles: bytesToB64(rleTiles(st, TILE_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL)),
275	    walls: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.wall)),
276	    liquid: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.liquid)),
277	    liquidType: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.liquidType)),
278	    wire: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.wire)),
279	    blocks: bytesToB64(rlePairs(combineBlocks(st.half, st.slope))),
280	    chests: meta.chests.map((c) => ({ x: c.x, y: c.y, locked: c.locked || undefined, items: c.items.map(mapItem) })),
281	    // v4:armor/dye/trash/miscEquips/miscDyes/loadouts 逐槽稳定 id(v3 是裸内部下标)
282	    player: {
283	      ...player,
284	      inventory: player.inventory.map(mapItem),
285	      banks: player.banks?.map((b) => b.map(mapItem)),
286	      armor: player.armor?.map(mapItem),
287	      dye: player.dye?.map(mapItem),
288	      trash: mapItem(player.trash ?? null),
289	      miscEquips: player.miscEquips?.map(mapItem),
290	      miscDyes: player.miscDyes?.map(mapItem),
291	      loadouts: player.loadouts?.map((l) => ({
292	        armor: l.armor.map(mapItem),
293	        dye: l.dye.map(mapItem),
294	      })),
295	    },
296	    npcs: townNpcs ?? [],   // 城镇 NPC 段(WorldFile.cs:1703-1750 NPC 段;曾硬编码 [] 断链)
297	    signs: meta.signs,
298	    // 挂物族：存放物 id 走与 chests 相同的稳定 id 映射（mapItem 同构，保留 prefix）
299	    furniture: meta.furniture?.map((e) => ({
300	      x: e.x, y: e.y, kind: e.kind,
301	      slots: e.slots.map((s) => (s ? { id: s.id === 0 ? 0 : ITEM_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL[s.id] ?? 0, stack: s.stack, prefix: s.prefix } : null)),
302	    })),
303	    flags: meta.flags,
304	    weather: meta.weather,
305	    events,
306	    bestiary: meta.bestiary,
307	    idScheme: 'sheet',
308	  };
309	  return JSON.stringify(data);
310	}
311	
312	/** half + slope → 单字节 blockType(原版 Tile.blockType: 0=整砖 1=半砖 2-5=slope1-4) */
313	function combineBlocks(half: Uint8Array, slope: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
314	  const out = new Uint8Array(half.length);
315	  for (let i = 0; i < half.length; i++) {
316	    out[i] = half[i] ? 1 : (slope[i] >= 1 && slope[i] <= 4 ? slope[i] + 1 : 0);
317	  }
318	  return out;
319	}
320	

```

```
export function loadSave(json: string): ReturnType<typeof loadSaveData> {
  return loadSaveData(JSON.parse(json) as SaveData);
}

/** 服务器侧世界-only 存档（P3.1 持久化）：tile/箱子/clock/flags/天气，不含玩家/城镇NPC。
 *  player 字段以空壳占位（格式必填；语义上服务器档无玩家，加载方忽略）。
 *  注意：serializeSave 的幽灵净化会就地写 store——服务器存储由 setTileSilent/
 *  loadSaveData 保持 type/flags 一致，该循环为 no-op，活存储不受影响。 */
export function saveWorldOnly(world: World): string {
  const st = world.store;
  const view: SaveStoreView = {
    type: st.type, flags: st.flags, frameX: st.frameX, frameY: st.frameY,
    wall: st.wall, liquid: st.liquid, liquidType: st.liquidType, wire: st.wire,
    half: st.half, slope: st.slope,
  };
  const meta: SaveMeta = {
    name: world.name, seed: world.seed, w: st.w, h: st.h,
    spawnX: world.spawnX, spawnY: world.spawnY,
    groundLevel: world.groundLevel, rockLevel: world.rockLevel, lavaLine: world.lavaLine,
    treeX: world.treeX, treeStyle: world.treeStyle, treeTops: world.treeTops,
    crimson: world.crimson, dungeonX: world.dungeonX, dungeonY: world.dungeonY, jungleX: world.jungleX,
    timeOfDay: world.clock.timeOfDay, dayCount: world.clock.dayCount,
    difficulty: world.difficulty,   // Main.GameMode（Main.cs:2677）——服务器档随世界下发
    chests: world.chests, signs: world.signs, flags: world.flags,
    weather: weatherToSave(world.weather),
    invasion: { type: world.invasionType, size: world.invasionSize, sizeStart: world.invasionSizeStart,
      x: world.invasionX, delay: world.invasionDelay },
    altarCount: world.altarCount,
    sundialCooldown: world.sundialCooldown, moondialCooldown: world.moondialCooldown,
    fastForwardTimeToDawn: world.fastForwardTimeToDawn,
    fastForwardTimeToDusk: world.fastForwardTimeToDusk,
    bestiary: world.bestiary.toSave(),  // BestiaryUnlocksTracker（WorldFile.cs:3399）
    // 挂物族存放物：内部 id 直传（serializeSave 内统一过稳定 id 表，与 chests 同路径）
    furniture: world.furnitureItems,
  };
  const placeholderPlayer: SaveData['player'] = {
    hp: 0, x: world.spawnX * 16, y: world.spawnY * 16,
    inventory: [], selected: 0,
  };
  return serializeSave(view, meta, placeholderPlayer, 0);

```

```
---
// worldGen worker ↔ 主线程协议：世界数据包（packWorld 打包 / World.fromPacket 重建）
// + 生成/读档请求与事件联合。（type-only 模块——运行时消息按此形状对齐）
import type { SaveData } from '../save/serialize';
import type { ChestData } from '../world/World';
import type { WeatherSave } from '../world/weather/Weather';
import type { BestiarySave } from '../data/Bestiary';
import type { FurnitureItemRecord } from '../world/FurnitureItems';

/** tile 存储的转移所有权 buffer 组（worker postMessage transfer 零拷贝） */
export interface TileBuffers {
  type: ArrayBuffer; flags: ArrayBuffer;
  frameX: ArrayBuffer; frameY: ArrayBuffer;
  wall: ArrayBuffer; liquid: ArrayBuffer; liquidType: ArrayBuffer;
  half: ArrayBuffer; slope: ArrayBuffer; wire: ArrayBuffer;
  /** 战争迷雾（旧包可缺省——fromPacket 以全零补齐；saveGame 不持久化 explored） */
  explored?: ArrayBuffer;
}

export interface WorldPacket {
  w: number; h: number; seed: number; name: string;
  spawnX: number; spawnY: number;
  groundLevel: number; rockLevel: number; lavaLine: number;
  crimson: boolean; dungeonX: number; dungeonY: number; jungleX: number;
  exploredVersion: number;
  /** 世界难度 = Main.GameMode（0 经典 1 专家 2 大师 3 旅程；旧包缺省 0） */
  difficulty?: number;
  clock: { timeOfDay: number; dayCount: number; bloodMoon?: boolean; eclipse?: boolean; moonPhase?: number };
  /** 月亮贴图样式（原版 header moonType；旧包缺省 0） */
  moonType?: number;
  /** 入侵五元组（生成路径恒 0；读档路径经本包回传——packWorld 曾漏传致 worker 读档丢入侵） */
  invasion?: { type: number; size: number; sizeStart: number; x: number; delay: number };
  /** 已砸祭坛数（读档路径回传;旧包缺省 0） */
  altarCount?: number;
  /** 日晷/月晷快进四件套（WorldFile.cs:1311/1342/1416-1417；旧包缺省=无快进无冷却） */
  sundial?: number; moondial?: number;
  fastForwardTimeToDawn?: boolean; fastForwardTimeToDusk?: boolean;
  /** 彩蛋种子旗标（SeedEasterEggs.parseSeed；血月 1/6 十周年 / drunkWorld 黎明翻转；旧包缺省={}） */
  seedFlags?: import('../world/gen/SeedEasterEggs').SeedFlags;
  /** 天气快照（读档路径回传;旧包缺省=晴） */
  weather?: WeatherSave;
  /** 图鉴三桶（读档路径回传;旧包缺省=空） */
  bestiary?: BestiarySave;
  /** 城镇 NPC 段（读档路径回传,Game.loadWorld 消费;旧包缺省=空→新世界出生分支） */
  pendingTownNpcs?: Array<{ key: string; x: number; y: number; home: [number, number] | null; bound?: boolean; givenName?: string }>;
  chests: ChestData[];
  signs: Array<{ x: number; y: number; text: string }>;
  /** 挂物族存放物（物品框/武器架/模特/帽架/食物盘/展示罐；生成路径无、读档路径有） */
  furnitureItems?: FurnitureItemRecord[];
  trees: Array<{ x: number; y: number; h: number }>;
  flags: Record<string, boolean>;
  treeX: number[]; treeStyle: number[]; treeTops: number[];
  /** 以太生物群系中心（无微光世界为 0；旧包可缺省） */
  shimmerX?: number; shimmerY?: number;
  buf: TileBuffers;
}

/** 生成配置（主线程 → worker；GenClient.generate 入参直传） */
export interface GenConfigDTO {
  width: number; height: number;
  seedText: string;
  name?: string;
  evil?: 0 | 1 | -1;  // 0 腐化 / 1 猩红 / -1 随机
  preview: boolean;   // 生成期降采样位图预览开关
}

export type WorldWorkerRequest =
  | { id: number; type: 'ping' }
  | { id: number; type: 'generate'; cfg: GenConfigDTO }
  | { id: number; type: 'saveParse'; key?: string; json?: string; save?: SaveData };
```

```
// 原版世界生成里凡显式 `new FastRandom(seed).WithModifier(...)` 的掷骰走这里
// （DesertHive.PlaceClustersArea 的 shell 类型骰/0.25 门、光照等）——绝不可用
// RNG/UnifiedRandom 代替，否则流偏移不可查。光照系统的副本在
// src/lighting/FastRandom.ts（同源、API 子集），此处是世界生成权威实现。
const FR_MULTIPLIER = 25214903917n;
const FR_ADD = 11n;
const FR_MASK = 0xffffffffffffn; // 2^48 - 1

export class FastRandom {
  private seed: bigint;

  constructor(seed: bigint | number) {
    this.seed = (typeof seed === 'bigint' ? seed : BigInt(seed)) & FR_MASK;
  }

  /** NextSeed(L85-88)：静态推进，不动自身状态 */
  static nextSeed(seed: bigint): bigint {
    return (seed * FR_MULTIPLIER + FR_ADD) & FR_MASK;
  }

  /** WithModifier(ulong)(L27-30)：新实例种子 = NextSeed(m) ^ 原 Seed（不改自身） */
  withModifier(m: number | bigint): FastRandom {
    const mod = typeof m === 'bigint' ? m : BigInt(m);
    return new FastRandom(FastRandom.nextSeed(mod) ^ this.seed);
  }

  /** WithModifier(int x, int y)(L32-35)：
   *  modifier = (ulong)(x + 2654435769u + ((long)y << 6)) + ((ulong)y >> 2)
   *  坑：C# 的 (ulong)y 是 64 位补码（负 y → 巨值再右移），不能写 JS 的
   *  `y >>> 0`（32 位截断）——DesertHive 的 hj=-20 行曾因此整行门骰错位 */
  withModifierXY(x: number, y: number): FastRandom {
    const u64y = BigInt(y) & 0xffffffffffffffffn;   // (ulong)y：64 位补码
    const mod = BigInt(x + 2654435769 + (y << 6)) + (u64y >> 2n);
    return this.withModifier(mod);
  }

  /** NextBits(b)(L47-51)：前进一步并取高 b 位 */
  private nextBits(bits: number): number {
    this.seed = FastRandom.nextSeed(this.seed);
    return Number(this.seed >> BigInt(48 - bits));
  }

  /** Next(max)(L63-78)（= Next(min,max) 的 range 部分）；2 的幂走快速路径，
   *  其余拒绝采样——溢出带判据 num <= 2^31-1-(max-1) 与原版 int32 回绕等价 */
  nextMax(max: number): number {
    if ((max & -max) === max) {
      // (int)((long)max * NextBits(31) >> 31)
      return Math.floor((max * this.nextBits(31)) / 2147483648);
    }
    for (;;) {
      const num = this.nextBits(31);          // [0, 2^31)
      const rem = num % max;
      if (num <= 2147483647 - (max - 1)) return rem;
    }
  }
```

```
// 确定性随机数：Terraria 1.4.5.6 位级对齐。
//
// ── UnifiedRandom（Terraria.Utilities/UnifiedRandom.cs 1:1）─────────────────
// .NET System.Random 的 Knuth 减法生成器（MSEED=161803398），纯 int32 运算。
// 位级移植要点（JS/C# 数值差异全部钉死，改动前先读）：
//  1. C# int 溢出 unchecked 回绕 ↔ JS：本实现所有中间值都落在 int32 范围内
//     （diff 最小 -(2^31-1)，`+((n>>31)&0x7FFFFFFF)` 归位后 ∈[0,2^31-1]），
//     无需模拟回绕；存储用 Int32Array 与 C# int[] 同构。
//  2. `(int)double` 是向零截断 ↔ JS Math.trunc（Sample()≥0 时与 floor 等价，
//     但必须写 trunc 以防有人把 internalSample 改出负值）。
//  3. `Sample()` 常数 4.656612875245797E-10 = 1/(2^31-1)，十进制字面量两语言
//     解析为同一 double，乘法 IEEE754 位级一致。
//  4. `Next(min,max)` 的 range 用 long（≤2^32）↔ JS number 精确整数，安全。
//     大 range 分支的 `(int)(long+min)` unchecked 回绕 ↔ JS `|0`（ToInt32 mod 2^32）。
//  5. `GetSampleForLargeRange` 的 `% 2 == 0`：C# 余数带符号但只判零，JS `%` 同判。
//  金标：tests/golden/unified-random.json 由真·Terraria.exe 反射导出
//  （tools/golden/rand-golden.cs），tests/unified-random.test.ts 位级断言。
//
// ── 种子解析（WorldFileData.TranslateSeed, cs:399-410 1:1）────────────────
// 数字字符串 → Math.Abs(int)（int.MinValue→int.MaxValue）；非数字 →
// ReLogic.Utilities.Crc32.Calculate（IEEE 表，按 UTF-16 code unit 逐字符，
// 注意 `(crc ^ c) & 0xFF` 只取 char 低字节）。Crc32 结果可为负，直接作种子。
export class UnifiedRandom {
  private seedArray: Int32Array;
  private inext = 0;

  constructor(seed: number) {
    this.seedArray = new Int32Array(56);
    this.setSeed(seed);
  }

  setSeed(seed: number): void {
```

````
Parsing strategy: a lightweight tokenizer over the method bodies. Each statement is one of:
- `AddStopper(X);` or `AddStopper(ocean);` (alias)
- `AFishingCondition desert = Desert;` alias decl
- `AddWithHardmode(Rarity, D, early, hard, conds...)`
- `AddQuestFishForRemix(Rarity, D, item, conds...)`
- `AddQuestFish(Rarity, D, item, conds...)`
- `Add(Rarity, [N,] D, item|Group(...), conds...)`
- `AFishingCondition[] array = Join(InLava, CanFishInLava);`
- `rolledHallowDesert = Hallow;` reassignment

Condition args can be: plain name, alias var, or `Join(array, Crate)` / `Join(array, HardMode)` / `Join(conditions...)`. Note AddQuestFish internally Joins the quest condition — handled by the extractor (adds questFish cond).

Item args: number literal, or `Group(a,b,c)` (with duplicate entries allowed: Group(2303, 2309, 2309, 2309)).

Let me now write the parser carefully. I'll parse statement-by-statement with regexes since the source is very regular (decompiled). The file uses tabs; each statement is on one line.

For the statement regexes, I'll split each method body into lines, strip trailing `;`, and dispatch.

Argument splitting needs to handle nested parens (Group(...), Join(...)). I'll write a splitArgs function.

For each generated rule, record:
- group: method name (e.g. 'LavaDrops')
- src: line number in GameContentFishDropPopulator.cs (and for AddWithHardmode the second rule gets +0 same line)
- rarity: 'Any'|'Legendary'|'VeryRare'|'Rare'|'Uncommon'|'Common'|'BombRarityOfNotLegendaryAndNotVeryRareAndUncommon'|'UncommonOrCommon'
- num, den
- items: number[] (empty for stoppers)
- conditions: array of normalized condition objects (each with its own src from the definition table)

Then the JSON:

{
  "_meta": { source: '...', generatedAt, ruleCount, ... },
  "conditions": {...defs with src...},
  "rules": [...]
}
```
````

```
  /** 放置一名 bound 救援 NPC（已解救/已在场/落点无效时跳过）。x,y = 脚底上一格 */
  private placeBoundNpc(key: string, savedFlag: string, x: number, y: number): boolean {
    const st = this.world.store;
    if (this.world.flags[savedFlag]) return false;
    if (this.entities.npcs.some((n) => n instanceof TownNPC && !n.dead && n.npcKey === key)) return false;
    if (x < 3 || y < 3 || x > st.w - 4 || y > st.h - 4) return false;
    if (st.isSolid(x, y) || st.isSolid(x, y - 1)) return false;
    const npc = new TownNPC(key, x * TILE + 8, (y + 1) * TILE);
    npc.bound = true;
    this.entities.add(npc, 'npcs');
    return true;
  }

  /** 右键解救 bound NPC（原版 bound 类型右键 → 转正 + AI_007_UpdateSavedStates 置 saved 旗标） */
  private freeBoundNpc(npc: TownNPC): void {
    npc.bound = false;
    const flagByKey: Record<string, string> = {
      goblin_tinkerer: 'savedGoblin', wizard: 'savedWizard', mechanic: 'savedMech',
      stylist: 'savedStylist', tax_collector: 'savedTaxCollector', golfer: 'savedGolfer',
    };
    const flag = flagByKey[npc.npcKey];
    if (flag) this.world.flags[flag] = true;
    const name = Lang.npcName(npc.vanillaId) ?? npc.npcKey;
    this.newText(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.NPC.Rescued', name), 50, 125, 255);
    this.spawnParticles(npc.cx, npc.cy, '#9AE85A', 14, 2);
    this.playSfxFiles(['NPC_Hit_1'], 0.6);
  }

  /** 粉末云实效（PowderProj 每帧回调；原版 AI_006 cs:22460-22533 逐格 Convert +
   *  Damage_TryUsingPowders cs:13646-13680 NPC 转化）：
   *  proj 10 净化 → Convert case 11；11 → 腐化 1；463 → 猩红 4（均 tiles+walls）
   *  NPC 转化：10 命中 bound 税务官（原版地狱酷刑者 534 → Transform 441，:13651-13655）
   *  → 走 freeBoundNpc 转正。687 城镇史莱姆黄 → 683 链无对应实体（未实装，登记）；
   *  11/463 的 AttemptToConvertNPCToEvil（兔/企鹅击杀等）未接（登记） */
  applyPowder(projId: number, x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number): void {
    const convType = projId === 10 ? 11 : projId === 11 ? 1 : projId === 463 ? 4 : -1;
    if (convType > 0) {
      const st = this.world.store;
      // 命中盒外扩 ±1 格的遍历窗（cs:22460-22466），窗内再做盒-格矩形相交判定
      const x0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(x / TILE) - 1);
      const x1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, Math.floor((x + w) / TILE) + 1);
      const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(y / TILE) - 1);
      const y1 = Math.min(st.h - 1, Math.floor((y + h) / TILE) + 1);
      for (let i = x0; i <= x1; i++) {
        for (let j = y0; j <= y1; j++) {
          const tx = i * TILE, ty = j * TILE;
          if (x + w <= tx || x >= tx + TILE || y + h <= ty || y >= ty + TILE) continue;
          this.evolution.convert(i, j, convType);
        }
      }
    }
    if (projId !== 10) return;
    for (const n of this.entities.npcs) {
      const npc = n as TownNPC;
      if (!(npc instanceof TownNPC) || npc.dead || !npc.bound) continue;
      if (npc.npcKey !== 'tax_collector') continue; // 原版 534 是唯一受净化粉转化的 NPC
      if (x < npc.x + npc.w && x + w > npc.x && y < npc.y + npc.h && y + h > npc.y) {
        this.freeBoundNpc(npc);
      }
    }
  }

  /** 守卫老人(NPC 37)生成/重生（原版 dungeonX/Y = 入口站立点 cs L72510-72527）。

```

```
  private updateTownNpcArrival() {
    // 入侵/月事件进行中暂停城镇 NPC 入驻（SpawnHomelessNPC :4987 门；月事件原版经
    // !dayTime 间接压制——本入驻轮无昼夜门，显式补 moonEventActive）
    if (invasionActive(this.world) || moonEventActive(this.world)) return;
    const npcs = this.entities.npcs.filter((n) => n instanceof TownNPC && !n.dead) as TownNPC[];
    const has = (key: string) => npcs.some((n) => n.npcKey === key);
    // npcId 随 home 携带（ScoreRoom 占用豁免 CanNPCsLiveWithEachOther 用）
    const homes = npcs.filter((n) => n.home)
      .map((n) => ({ x: n.home!.x, y: n.home!.y, npcId: n.vanillaId }));
    const st = this.world.store;

    // ①' 困难模式开启后补放被绑巫师（原版仅困难世界放置）
    if (this.world.flags.hardMode) {
      const s = this.findBoundSpotByDepth(Math.floor(this.world.rockLevel) + 60, st.h - 350);
      if (s) this.placeBoundNpc('wizard', 'savedWizard', s[0], s[1]);
    }

    // ① QuickFindHome（Main.cs:65072：每轮对未安家 NPC 找房入住）
    // bound（被绑待救）不入住不搬动——原版 AI_007 bound 态原地，否则五个救援 NPC
    // 会被塞进同一间空房叠在一点（读档/新世界 7200 tick 后必现）
    for (const n of npcs) {
      if (n.home || n.bound || n.npcKey === 'old_man') continue;
      const house = findFreeHouse(st, homes, this.world.spawnX, this.world.spawnY, n.vanillaId);
      if (!house) break;
      n.home = { x: house.standX, y: house.standY };
      n.leashHome = true; // 有家者白天在家附近游走
      homes.push({ x: house.standX, y: house.standY, npcId: n.vanillaId });
      n.x = house.standX * TILE + 8 - n.w / 2;
      n.y = (house.standY + 1) * TILE - n.h;
    }

    // ② 优先级链（Main.cs num42 完整顺序；护士/爆破手要求商人在场，
    //    染料商 ≥4 人、油漆工 ≥8 人、派对女孩 ≥20 人且 1/40 重掷）
    const merchantHere = has('merchant');
    const allow = (key: string) => this.townNpcSpawnAllowed(key);
    const n = npcs.length; // num40：在场城镇 NPC 总数（含守卫老人，原版同）
    const prio: Array<[string, boolean]> = [
      ['guide', !has('guide')],
      ['merchant', !merchantHere && allow('merchant')],
      ['nurse', !has('nurse') && merchantHere && allow('nurse')],
      ['arms_dealer', !has('arms_dealer') && allow('arms_dealer')],
      ['goblin_tinkerer', !has('goblin_tinkerer') && allow('goblin_tinkerer')],
      ['wizard', !has('wizard') && allow('wizard')],
      ['dryad', !has('dryad') && allow('dryad')],
      ['demolitionist', !has('demolitionist') && merchantHere && allow('demolitionist')],
      ['witch_doctor', !has('witch_doctor') && allow('witch_doctor')],
      ['steampunker', !has('steampunker') && allow('steampunker')],
      ['mechanic', !has('mechanic') && allow('mechanic')],
      ['stylist', !has('stylist') && allow('stylist')],
      ['cyborg', !has('cyborg') && allow('cyborg')],
      ['pirate', !has('pirate') && allow('pirate')],
      ['clothier', !has('clothier') && allow('clothier')],
      ['dyer', !has('dyer') && n >= 4 && allow('dyer')],
      ['painter', !has('painter') && n >= 8],
      ['party_girl', !has('party_girl') && n >= 20 && allow('party_girl')],
      ['santa_claus', !has('santa_claus') && allow('santa_claus')],
      ['golfer', !has('golfer') && allow('golfer')],
      ['tax_collector', !has('tax_collector') && allow('tax_collector')],
      ['truffle', !has('truffle') && allow('truffle')],
      ['zoologist', !has('zoologist') && allow('zoologist')],
      ['princess', !has('princess') && allow('princess')],
    ];
    const pick = prio.find(([, ok]) => ok)?.[0];
    if (!pick) return;

    // ③ 入住 / 流浪生成（askingNpcId = 待生成者，占用豁免/同房避让用）
    const id = TOWN_NPC_IDS[pick].id;
    const house = findFreeHouse(st, homes, this.world.spawnX, this.world.spawnY, id);
    const name = Lang.npcName(id) ?? pick;
    if (house) {
      // SpawnTownNPC：房间合格且无人 → 生成在房内、安家（Announcement.HasArrived）
      const npc = new TownNPC(pick, house.standX * TILE + 8, (house.standY + 1) * TILE);
      npc.home = { x: house.standX, y: house.standY };
      npc.leashHome = true;
      this.entities.add(npc, 'npcs');
      this.newText(Lang.text('Announcement.HasArrived', name), 50, 125, 255);
    } else {
      // SpawnHomelessNPC（:4992）：白天且当前无流浪 NPC 时，在已入住者的家附近生成；
      // 无人入住（无锚点）则不生成——新世界只有向导（WorldGen.cs:20036 出生）
      if (!this.world.clock.isDay || homes.length === 0) return;
      if (npcs.some((n) => !n.home)) return;
      const anchor = homes[Math.floor(Math.random() * homes.length)];
      const ax = anchor.x + (Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1) * (30 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 50));
      if (ax < 3 || ax > st.w - 4) return;
      let ay = Math.max(4, anchor.y - 20);
      const limit = Math.min(st.h - 3, anchor.y + 20);
      while (ay < limit && !st.isSolid(ax, ay)) ay++;
      if (ay >= limit) return;
      const npc = new TownNPC(pick, ax * TILE + 8, (ay + 1) * TILE);
      this.entities.add(npc, 'npcs');
      this.newText(Lang.text(`Announcement.HomelessArrived_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 6)}`, name), 50, 125, 255);
    }
  }

  /** 右键与附近 NPC 交谈(Player.SetTalkNPC Player.cs:4180 语义:
 *  光标命中 NPC 命中盒(±8px)+ 距离 3.5 格内)→ 打开原版对话框 */
  private talkToNearbyNpc(ctX: number, ctY: number): boolean {
    const p = this.player;
    for (const n of this.entities.npcs) {
      const npc = n as TownNPC;
      if (Math.hypot(npc.cx - p.cx, npc.cy - p.cy) > TILE * 3.5) continue;
      const mx = (ctX + 0.5) * TILE, my = (ctY + 0.5) * TILE;
      if (mx < npc.x - 8 || mx > npc.x + npc.w + 8 || my < npc.y - 8 || my > npc.y + npc.h + 8) continue;
      if (npc.bound) { this.freeBoundNpc(npc); return true; } // 救援 NPC：右键直接解救
      this.openNpcDialog(npc);
```

````
function parseMethod(src, name) {
  const start = src.indexOf(`private void ${name}()`);
  if (start < 0) throw new Error(`method ${name} not found`);
  let i = src.indexOf('{', start);
  let depth = 1; i++;
  while (depth > 0 && i < src.length) {
    const ch = src[i];
    if (ch === '{') depth++;
    else if (ch === '}') depth--;
    i++;
  }
  return src.slice(start, i); // includes signature
}

- heightLevel, X (bobber tile x), Y, waterTilesCount, rockLevel (for UnderRockLayer), maxTilesX (for Ocean), inLava/inHoney/junk/crate/legendary/etc, questFish, rolledEnemySpawn>0 (anyEnemies), CanFishInLava, zones from PLAYER (zoneDungeon && downedBoss3, zoneBeach, zoneHallow, zoneGlowshroom, zoneDesert(TrueDesert), zoneSnow(TrueSnow)), rolled flags (RolledCorruption etc.), remix (false — no remix worlds; note as unsupported), bloodMoon, combatBookWasUsed.

Note the Ocean condition uses `context.Fisher.heightLevel <= 1 && (X < 380 || X > maxTilesX - 380) && waterTilesCount > 1000` — the existing zoneOcean in Bobber used `y < w.groundLevel` which is WRONG (should be heightLevel <= 1, i.e. Y < worldSurface). Since heightLevel 0/1 both mean Y < worldSurface, `heightLevel <= 1` ≡ `Y < worldSurface`. OK equivalent.

PrepareFishingContext (:19451-19502):
```
flag = Player.ZoneCorrupt; flag2 = ZoneCrimson; flag3 = ZoneJungle; flag4 = ZoneSnow; flag5 = ZoneDungeon && downedBoss3
if (!downedBoss3) flag5 = false
if (notTheBees && !remix && Next(2)==0) flag3 = false
if (remix && heightLevel==0) {flag=false; flag2=false}
else if (flag && flag2) { if (Next(2)==0) flag2=false else flag=false }
if (flag4 && flag3 && Next(2)==0) flag4=false
flag6 = ZoneDesert; if (flag5) flag6=false
rolledRemixOcean = remix && heightLevel==1 && Y>=rockLayer && Next(3)==0
RolledInfectedDesert = flag6 && Next(2)==0
```
I'll implement this 1:1 (notTheBees → false in our world; remix → false).

Now, `RolledHallowDesert = RolledInfectedDesert && Player.ZoneHallow` (:147).

Let me now write all the code. Start with the extraction script.

Also I need to double check where Main.rand vs context.Random is used in the roll: the rule list uses context.Random (static shared). Fine, Math.random equivalent.

One subtlety in FishDropRule.Attempt: `if (context.Random.Next(ChanceDenominator) >= ChanceNumerator) return false;` — chance roll BEFORE rarity. Then `if (!Rarity.Matches(context)) return false;`. Then pick item. Order: conditions → chance → rarity → item. My implementation must follow this exactly (affects nothing statistically since both are independent uniform rolls, but faithful anyway).

Let me write the extractor now.
````

```
Hmm, but `(?: \|\| num == (\d+))* \|\| num == (\d+))?` — inside the optional group, after `num == (\d+)` we have `(?: \|\| num == (\d+))*` (zero or more) then ` \|\| num == (\d+)` (MANDATORY one). So the optional group requires at least one ` || num == N` after the first! That's the bug — for single-item conditions the optional group can't match, so the whole `if (...)` requires... wait, the group is optional (`?`), so if it fails, the group is skipped entirely and then ` && ` must match right after `if (`. But then the text starts with `num == 2454 && ...` — ` && ` doesn't match `num ==...`. So no match for singles.

Fix: restructure to `/if \((num == (\d+)(?: \|\| num == (\d+))*) && ([^)]*(?:\([^)]*\))?)\)/`. Let me rewrite that part.
```

</details>

else corrupt = false;   // :19473-19480\n  }\n  // 雪∩丛林 1/2 弃雪（:19482-19485）\n  if (snow && jungle && rand(2) === 0) snow = false;\n  const desert = zones.desert;                 // flag6 :19486\n  // remixOcean（:19491）：remix && heightLevel==1 && y>=rockLayer && Next(3)==0 —— remix 恒 false\n  return {\n    corruption: corrupt, crimson, jungle, snow, desert,\n    infectedDesert: desert && rand(2) === 0,   // :19499\n    remixOcean: false,\n  };\n}\n\n// ==================== 规则表求值（FishDropRuleList.cs:10-21 + FishDropRule.cs:31-51）====================\n\n/** 条件求值（AFishDropRulePopulator.cs 各字段 lambda 的归一化实现，行号见 cond.src） */\nfunction condMatches(c: FishCond, ctx: FishingRollCtx): boolean {\n  switch (c.t) {\n    case 'hardMode': return ctx.hardMode === !!c.v;\n    case 'inLava': return ctx.inLava;\n    case 'inHoney': return ctx.inHoney;\n    case 'junk': return ctx.junk;\n    case 'crate': return ctx.crate;\n    case 'anyEnemies': return ctx.anyEnemies;\n    case 'canFishInLava': return ctx.canFishInLava;\n    case 'dungeon': return ctx.zones.dungeon;\n    case 'beach': return ctx.zones.desert === false && false || zoneBeachOf(ctx); // 见下：原式 = Player.ZoneBeach\n    case 'hallow': return ctx.zones.hallow;\n    case 'glowshroom': return zoneGlowshroomOf(ctx);\n    case 'trueDesert': return ctx.zones.desert;\n    case 'trueSnow': return ctx.zones.snow;\n    case 'remix': return ctx.remix;\n    case 'height': {\n      const h = ctx.heightLevel;\n      switch (c.cmp) {\n        case '==': return h === c.v;\n        case '>': return h > (c.v as number);\n        case '>=': return h >= (c.v as number);\n        case '<': return h < (c.v as number);\n        case 'in': return (c.v as number[]).includes(h);\n        default: return false;\n      }\n    }\n    case 'underRockLayer': return ctx.y >= ctx.rockLayer;\n    case 'rolledCorruption': return ctx.rolled.corruption;\n    case 'rolledCrimson': return ctx.rolled.crimson;\n    case 'rolledJungle': return ctx.rolled.jungle;\n    case 'rolledSnow': return ctx.rolled.snow;\n    case 'rolledDesert': return ctx.rolled.desert;\n    case 'rolledHallowDesert': return ctx.rolled.infectedDesert && ctx.zones.hallow;                // :153\n    case 'water1000': return ctx.waterTilesCount > 1000;\n    case 'bloodMoon': return ctx.bloodMoon;\n    case 'didNotUseCombatBook': return !ctx.combatBookWasUsed;\n    case 'questFish': return ctx.questFish === c.item;                                 // FishingConditions.cs:5-13\n    case 'questFishRemix': return ctx.questFish === c.item && ctx.remix;               // FishingConditions.cs:15-27\n    default: return false;\n  }\n}\n\n/** IsOriginalOcean（AFishDropRulePopulator.cs:257-264）：heightLevel ≤ 1\n *  且 横向边缘 380 格内 且 水量 > 1000 */\nfunction isOriginalOcean(ctx: FishingRollCtx): boolean {\n  return ctx.heightLevel <= 1 && (ctx.x < 380 || ctx.x > ctx.maxTilesX - 380) && ctx.waterTilesCount > 1000;\n}\n\n// beach / glowshroom 不在 ZoneInput（SceneMetrics 有但规则表未直接消费 beach 的\n// 玩家态以外的语义）——经可选扩展位透传，缺省 false\nfunction zoneBeachOf(ctx: FishingRollCtx): boolean {\n  return (ctx.zones as ZoneInput & { beach?: boolean }).beach ?? false;\n}\nfunction zoneGlowshroomOf(ctx: FishingRollCtx): boolean {\n  return (ctx.zones as ZoneInput & { glowshroom?: boolean }).glowshroom ?? false;\n}\n\n/** 稀有档命中（AFishDropRulePopulator.cs:41-83 Rarity 表） */\nfunction rarityMatches(rarity: string, ctx: FishingRollCtx): boolean {\n  switch (rarity) {\n    case 'Any': return true;\n    case 'Legendary': return ctx.legendary;\n    case 'VeryRare': return ctx.veryrare;\n    case 'Rare': return ctx.rare;\n    case 'Uncommon': return ctx.uncommon;\n    case 'Common': return ctx.common;\n    case 'UncommonOrCommon': return ctx.uncommon || ctx.common;\n    case 'BombRarityOfNotLegendaryAndNotVeryRareAndUncommon':\n      return !ctx.legendary && !ctx.veryrare && ctx.uncommon;\n    default: return false;\n  }\n}\n\n/** FishDropRuleList.TryGetItemDropType（FishDropRuleList.cs:10-21）1:1：\n *  按注册序逐条 Attempt，第一条命中即返回其物品 id；全表落空返回 0。\n *  单条 Attempt（FishDropRule.cs:31-51）：条件 → chance（Next(den) < num）→ 稀有档 → 池取一。 */\nexport function tryGetItemDrop(ctx: FishingRollCtx): number {\n  const rand = ctx.rand ?? defaultRand;\n  for (let i = 0; i < FISH_DROP_RULES.length; i++) {\n    const rule = FISH_DROP_RULES[i];\n    let ok = true;\n    for (const c of rule.conditions) {              // MeetsConditions（:53-64）\n      if (!condMatches(c, ctx)) { ok = false; break; }\n    }\n    if (!ok) continue;\n    if (rand(rule.den) >= rule.num) continue;       // :38-41（chance 先于稀有档）\n    if (!rarityMatches(rule.rarity, ctx)) continue; // :42-45\n    if (rule.items.length === 0) return 0;          // Stopper：Attempt 返回 true 但无物品\n    return rule.items[rand(rule.items.length)];     // NextFromList（:46-49）\n  }\n  return 0;\n}\n\n// ==================== 渔夫任务链：每日选取（Main.cs:3799-3947）====================\n\n/** AnglerQuestSwap 的世界门（Main.cs:3807-3866 逐条 if 的归一化形，见 json filters） */\nexport interface AnglerQuestGates {\n  hardMode: boolean;\n  /** 世界邪恶类型（WorldGen.crimson） */\n  evil: 'corrupt' | 'crimson';\n  /** downedBoss1||downedBoss2||downedBoss3||hardMode||downedSlimeKing||downedQueenBee（:3807） */\n  anyBoss: boolean;\n  /** Main.isThereAWorldSurface（Main.cs:2861 = worldSurface > 50） */\n  hasWorldSurface: boolean;\n}\n\nfunction gatesPass(gates: Array<{ gate: string; want: boolean | string }>, g: AnglerQuestGates): boolean {\n  return gates.every((x) => {\n    switch (x.gate) {\n      case 'hardMode': return g.hardMode === x.want;\n      case 'evil': return g.evil === x.want;\n      case 'anyBoss': return g.anyBoss === !!x.want;\n      default: return false;\n    }\n  });\n}\n\n/** Main.AnglerQuestSwap（Main.cs:3799-3947）的选取循环 1:1：\n *  Next(41) 取索引 → 不满足世界门则重掷（拒绝表 json.anglerQuest.filters，逐条行号在\n *  条目 src）→ 无地表世界整族拒绝（noSurfaceItems）。返回 anglerQuest 索引。\n *  ★ 原版用 Main.rand（世界主随机流，不可复现）；此处注入独立 rand，调用方以\n *  （世界种子 × 天数）播种 → 同种子同天数同任务鱼（联机/读档一致）。 */\nexport function rollAnglerQuest(gates: AnglerQuestGates, rand: Rand = defaultRand): number {\n  const idx = data.anglerQuest.filters\n    .filter((f) => !gatesPass(f.gates, gates))\n    .flatMap((f) => f.items);\n  const banned = new Set(gates.hasWorldSurface ? idx : [...idx, ...data.anglerQuest.noSurfaceItems]);\n  for (;;) {\n    const q = rand(ANGLER_QUEST_ITEMS.length); stack: number }\n\n/** GetAnglerRewardRarityMultiplier（Player.cs:55363-55368）1:1：\n *  ≤50 次：1-0.01n；≤100：0.5-0.005(n-50)；≤150：0.25-0.002(n-100)；>150：0.15——再 ×0.9 */\nexport function anglerRewardRarityMultiplier(questsDone: number): number {\n  let m: number;\n  if (questsDone <= 50) m = 1 - questsDone * 0.01;\n  else if (questsDone <= 100) m = 0.5 - (questsDone - 50) * 0.005;\n  else if (questsDone > 150) m = 0.15;\n  else m = 0.25 - (questsDone - 100) * 0.002;\n  return m * 0.9;\n}\n\n/** RemoveAnglerAccOptionsFromRewardPool（Player.cs:55704-55741）的组合件表：\n *  玩家持有组合件 → 其全部零件从奖励池剔除 */\nconst ACC_COMBOS: Array<{ combo: number[]; parts: number[] }> = [\n  { combo: [3721, 5064], parts: [2373, 2375, 2374] },                        // :55713-55718\n  { combo: [3036, 3123, 3124, 5358, 5359, 5360, 5361], parts: [3120, 3037, 3096] }, // :55719-55729\n  { combo: [5140, 5141, 5142, 5143, 5144, 5145, 5146], parts: [5139] },      // :55730-55738\n];\n\nexport interface AnglerRewardOpts {\n  /** 已完成任务数（交付前置 ++ 后的值，Main.cs:39671） */\n  questsDone: number;\n  /** 本次交付的任务鱼（questItemType，GetAnglerReward :55351 入参） */\n  questItemType: number;\n  hardMode: boolean;\n  /** 玩家持有的物品类型全集（inventory/armor/bank×4/loadouts，DropAnglerAccByMissing :55647-55684） */\n  ownedTypes: Set<number>;\n  /** currentShoppingSettings.PriceAdjustment（:55356）——本仓无快乐度议价，恒 0 */\n  priceAdjustment?: number;\n  rand?: Rand;\n}\n\n/** Player.GetAnglerReward（:55351-55361）四段主干的纯函数移植。\n *  返回按原版顺序的发放列表（MainReward → Decoration → Money → Bait），\n *  2417/2498 随带件已展开（:55499-55516）。 */\nexport function rollAnglerRewards(opts: AnglerRewardOpts): AnglerRewardGrant[] {\n  const rand = opts.rand ?? defaultRand;\n  const { questsDone: n, hardMode } = opts;\n  let rarity = anglerRewardRarityMultiplier(n);\n  rarity *= ((opts.priceAdjustment ?? 0) + 1) / 2; i < 3; i++) p *= 1 - 1 / Math.floor(40 * rarity);   // :55408-55411\n      for (let j = 0; j < 3; j++) p *= 1 - 1 / Math.floor(30 * rarity);   // :55412-55415\n      p *= 1 - 1 / Math.floor(25 * rarity);                               // :55416\n      let accChance = (1 - p) * 0.8;                                      // :55417-55418\n      // 高阶奖励串（:55419-55468，先到先得）\n      if (n > 75 && rand(Math.floor(250 * rarity)) === 0) main = 2294; push(2419); }\n    else if (main === 2498) { push(2499); push(2500); }\n  }\n\n  // ---- Decoration（:55519-55552）----\n  {\n    const lerpTo = 1 - rarity;                                   // :55521 value = 1 - rarityReduction\n    const chance = lerpTo + (1 - lerpTo) * Math.min(1, n / 100); false）\n  };\n  void heightLevel; void y; void rockLayer;\n}\n\n// ==================== 规则表求值（FishDropRuleList.cs:10-21 + FishDropRule.cs:31-51）====================\n\n/** 条件求值（AFishDropRulePopulator.cs 各字段 lambda 的归一化实现，行号见 cond.src） */\nfunction condMatches(c: FishCond, ctx: FishingRollCtx): boolean {\n  switch (c.t) {\n    case 'hardMode': return ctx.hardMode === !!c.v;\n    case 'inLava': return ctx.inLava;\n    case 'inHoney': return ctx.inHoney;\n    case 'junk': return ctx.junk;\n    case 'crate': return ctx.crate;\n    case 'anyEnemies': return ctx.anyEnemies;\n    case 'canFishInLava': return ctx.canFishInLava;\n    case 'dungeon': return ctx.zones.dungeon;\n    case 'beach': return !!ctx.zones.beach;\n    case 'hallow': return ctx.zones.hallow;\n    case 'glowshroom': return !!ctx.zones.glowshroom;\n    case 'trueDesert': return ctx.zones.desert;\n    case 'trueSnow': return ctx.zones.snow;\n    case 'remix': return ctx.remix;\n    case 'height': {\n      const h = ctx.heightLevel;\n      switch (c.cmp) {\n        case '==': return h === c.v;\n        case '>': return h > (c.v as number);\n        case '>=': return h >= (c.v as number);\n        case '<': return h < (c.v as number);\n        case 'in': return (c.v as number[]).includes(h);\n        default: return false;\n      }\n    }\n    case 'underRockLayer': return ctx.y >= ctx.rockLayer;\n    case 'rolledCorruption': return ctx.rolled.corruption;\n    case 'rolledCrimson': return ctx.rolled.crimson;\n    case 'rolledJungle': return ctx.rolled.jungle;\n    case 'rolledSnow': return ctx.rolled.snow;\n    case 'rolledDesert': return ctx.rolled.desert;\n    case 'rolledHallowDesert': return ctx.rolled.infectedDesert && ctx.zones.hallow;  // :147\n    case 'originalOcean': return isOriginalOcean(ctx);                                 // :149/:257-264\n    case 'rolledRemixOcean': return ctx.rolled.remixOcean; 'bloodMoon': return ctx.bloodMoon;\n    case 'didNotUseCombatBook': return !ctx.combatBookWasUsed;\n    case 'questFish': return ctx.questFish === c.item;                                 // FishingConditions.cs:5-13\n    case 'questFishRemix': return ctx.questFish === c.item && ctx.remix;               // FishingConditions.cs:15-27\n    default: return false;\n  }\n}\n\n/** IsOriginalOcean（AFishDropRulePopulator.cs:257-264）：heightLevel ≤ 1\n *  且 横向边缘 380 格内 且 水量 > 1000 */\nfunction isOriginalOcean(ctx: FishingRollCtx): boolean {\n  return ctx.heightLevel <= 1 && (ctx.x < 380 || ctx.x > ctx.maxTilesX - 380) && ctx.waterTilesCount > 1000;\n}\n\n/** 稀有档命中（AFishDropRulePopulator.cs:41-83 Rarity 表） */\nfunction rarityMatches(rarity: string, ctx: FishingRollCtx): boolean {\n  switch (rarity) {\n    case 'Any': return true;\n    case 'Legendary': return ctx.legendary;\n    case 'VeryRare': return ctx.veryrare;\n    case 'Rare': return ctx.rare;\n    case 'Uncommon': return ctx.uncommon;\n    case 'Common': return ctx.common;\n    case 'UncommonOrCommon': return ctx.uncommon || ctx.common;\n    case 'BombRarityOfNotLegendaryAndNotVeryRareAndUncommon':\n      return !ctx.legendary && !ctx.veryrare && ctx.uncommon;   // :74\n    default: return false;\n  }\n}\n\n/** FishDropRuleList.TryGetItemDropType（FishDropRuleList.cs:10-21）1:1：\n *  按注册序逐条 Attempt，第一条命中即返回其物品 id；全表落空返回 0。\n *  单条 Attempt（FishDropRule.cs:31-51）：条件 → chance（Next(den) < num）→ 稀有档 → 池取一。 */\nexport function tryGetItemDrop(ctx: FishingRollCtx): number {\n  const rng = ctx.rng ?? mathRng;\n  for (let i = 0; i < FISH_DROP_RULES.length; i++) {\n    const rule = FISH_DROP_RULES[i];\n    let ok = true;\n    for (const c of rule.conditions) {              // MeetsConditions（:53-64）\n      if (!condMatches(c, ctx)) { ok = false; break; }\n    }\n    if (!ok) continue;\n    if (rng.next(rule.den) >= rule.num) continue;   // :38-41（chance 先于稀有档）\n    if (!rarityMatches(rule.rarity, ctx)) continue; // :42-45\n    if (rule.items.length === 0) return 0;          // Stopper：Attempt 返回 true 但无物品\n    return rule.items[rng.next(rule.items.length)]; // NextFromList（:46-49）\n  }\n  return 0;\n}\n\n// ==================== 渔夫任务链：每日选取（Main.cs:3799-3947）====================\n\n/** AnglerQuestSwap 的世界门（Main.cs:3807-3866 逐条 if 的归一化形，见 json filters） */\nexport interface AnglerQuestGates {\n  hardMode: boolean;\n  /** 世界邪恶类型（WorldGen.crimson） */\n  evil: 'corrupt' | 'crimson';\n  /** downedBoss1||downedBoss2||downedBoss3||hardMode||downedSlimeKing||downedQueenBee（:3807） */\n  anyBoss: boolean;\n  /** Main.isThereAWorldSurface（Main.cs:2861 = worldSurface > 50） */\n  hasWorldSurface: boolean;\n}\n\nfunction gatesPass(gates: Array<{ gate: string; want: boolean | string }>, g: AnglerQuestGates): boolean {\n  return gates.every((x) => {\n    switch (x.gate) {\n Main.AnglerQuestSwap 选取循环（Main.cs:3809-3945）1:1：\n *  Next(41) 取索引 → 命中拒绝表（世界门不满足）则重掷 → 无地表世界整族拒绝\n *  （noSurfaceItems）。返回 anglerQuest 索引。\n *  ★ 原版掷 Main.rand（世界主随机流，跨系统不可复现）；此处由调用方注入按\n *  「世界种子 × .filter((f) => !gatesPass(f.gates, gates))\n    .flatMap((f) => f.items);\n  if (!gates.hasWorldSurface) bannedItems.push(...data.anglerQuest.noSurfaceItems);   // :3870-3944\n  const banned = new GetAnglerRewardRarityMultiplier（Player.cs:55363-55368）1:1：\n *  ≤50 次：1-0.01n；≤100：0.5-0.005(n-50)；≤150：0.25-0.002(n-100)；>150：0.15——再 ×0.9 */\nexport function anglerRewardRarityMultiplier(questsDone: priceAdjustment?: number;\n  rng?: Rng;\n}\n\n/** Player.GetAnglerReward（:55351-55361）四段主干的纯函数移植。\n *  返回按原版顺序的发放列表（MainReward → Decoration → Money → Bait）；\n *  2417/2498 的随带件已展开（:55499-55516）。 rarity)) === 0) main = 2422; // :55424-55428\n    else if (opts.hardMode && n > 10 && rng.next(Math.floor(70 * rarity)) === 0) main = 2494;  // :55429-55433\n    else if (n > 10 && rng.next(Math.floor(70 * rarity)) === 0) main = 3031;                   // :55434-55438\n    else if (n > 10 && rng.next(Math.floor(70 * rarity)) === 0) main = 3032;                   // :55439-55443\n    else if (rng.next(Math.floor(80 * rarity)) === 0) main = 3183;                             // :55444-55448\n    else if (rng.next(Math.floor(60 * rarity)) === 0) main = 2360;    if (main === 2417) { push(2418); push(2419); }               // :55499-55507\n    else if (main === 2498) { push(2499); push(2500); }          // :55508-55516\n  }\n\n  // ---- ② Decoration（:55519-55552）----\n  {\n    const from = 1 - rarity;                                     // :55521 value = 1 - rarityReduction\n    const chance = from + (1 - from) * Math.min(1, n / 100); 银币\n      if (money > 99) money = 99;\n      if (money < 1) money = 1;\n      push(72, money);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // ---- ④ Bait（:55554-55597）----\n  if (rng.next(Math.floor(100 * rarity)) <= 50) {   nextInt(maxValue: number): number {
```
    return Math.trunc(this.sample() * maxValue);
  }

  /** C# NextDouble()（:113-115）*/
  nextDouble(): number {
    return this.sample();
  }

  /** C# Peek()（:88-99，非消耗）：下一 InternalSample 原始差值——对账/断点用 */
  peek(): number {
    let num = this.inext + 1;
    if (num > 55) num = 1;
    let num2 = num + 21;
    if (num2 > 55) num2 -= 55;
    return this.seedArray[num] - this.seedArray[num2];
  }
}
```

```json
 "new_string": "/** UnifiedRandom（core/rng.cs 1:1）种子实现——任务链按「世界种子 × 天数」播种 */\nexport function seededRng(seed: number): Rng {\n  const r = new UnifiedRandom(seed);\n  return {\n    next: (n) => (n <= 0 ? 0 : r.nextInt(n)),          // Next(maxValue)\n    range: (min, max) => (max <= min ? min : r.nextIntRange(min, max)),  // Next(min,max)\n    double: () => r.nextDouble(),                      // NextDouble()\n  };\n}"
}
```

```
    this.inHoney = liquidCollision(world.store, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, 3);
```
